is a weighted average of the gradient
\nabla F(z*\mathbf{x})
, namely,
\nabla F^{a}(\mathbf{x})\triangleq\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\nabla F_{t}(z*\boldsymbol{x})\mathrm{d}z
where
w(z)
is a positive weight function over
[0,1]
, we have that:

1):
When
f
is
\alpha
-weakly DR-submodular and
w(z)=e^{\alpha(z-1)}
, for any vector
(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and any subset
S
within the constraint of problem (
1
), then the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\Big\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}}-(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}),\nabla F^{a}(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\Big\rangle

(29)

\displaystyle=\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}}-(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}),\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left(1-e^{-\alpha}\right)f(S)-F(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K});

2):
When
f
is
(\gamma,\beta)
-weakly submodular and
w(z)=e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}
where
\phi(\gamma,\beta)=\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}
, for any policy vector
(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and any subset
S
within the constraint of problem (
1
), then the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\Big\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}}-(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}),\nabla F^{a}(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\Big\rangle

(30)

\displaystyle=\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}}-(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}),\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\right)f(S)-F(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}).

Proof.

For
1)
: At first, we verify the following relationship:

\displaystyle\left\langle(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}),\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z\right\rangle=\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\Big\langle(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}),\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\Big\rangle\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\frac{\mathrm{d}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)}{\mathrm{d}z}\mathrm{d}z=\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\mathrm{d}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)

\displaystyle=e^{\alpha(z-1)}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)|_{z=0}^{z=1}-\int_{0}^{1}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}\left(e^{\alpha(z-1)}\right)

\displaystyle=F(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})-\alpha\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z,

where the final equality follows from
F(\mathbf{0})=0
.

Then, we have the following inequality:

\displaystyle\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}},\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z\right\rangle=\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\Big\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}},\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\Big\rangle\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\geq\alpha\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\Big(f(S)-F\big(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\big)\Big)\mathrm{d}z=\Big(\alpha\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\mathrm{d}z\Big)f(S)-\alpha\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\Big(1-e^{-\alpha}\Big)f(S)-\alpha\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z,

where the first inequality follows from
Theorem

3
.

As a result, we have

\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}}-(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}),\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z\right\rangle\geq\left(1-e^{-\alpha}\right)f(S)-F(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}).

As for
ii):
Similarly, when
f
is monotone
(\gamma,\beta)
-weakly submodular and
w(z)=e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}
where
\phi(\gamma,\beta)=\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}),\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z\right\rangle=\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\Big\langle(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}),\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\Big\rangle\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\frac{\mathrm{d}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)}{\mathrm{d}z}\mathrm{d}z=\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\mathrm{d}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)

\displaystyle=e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)|_{z=0}^{z=1}-\phi(\gamma,\beta)\int_{0}^{1}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}\left(e^{\alpha(z-1)}\right)

\displaystyle=F(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})-\phi(\gamma,\beta)\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z,

where the final equality follows from
F(\mathbf{0})=0
.

Then, we have the following inequality:

\displaystyle\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}},\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z\right\rangle=\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\Big\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}},\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\Big\rangle\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\geq\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\Big(\gamma^{2}f(S)-\phi(\gamma,\beta)F\big(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\big)\Big)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\Big(\gamma^{2}\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\mathrm{d}z\Big)f(S)-\phi(\gamma,\beta)\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\gamma^{2}\left(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\right)}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\Big)f(S)-\phi(\gamma,\beta)\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z,

where the first inequality follows from
Theorem

3
.

As a result, we have

\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}}-(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}),\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z\right\rangle\geq\left(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\right)f(S)-F(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}).

∎

From
Definition

2
, we can show that, when the vector
(\mathbf{p}^{a}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}^{a}_{K})
is the stationary point of the surrogate objective
F_{a}
over domain
\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and
S
satisfies the constraint of problem (
1
), due to
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
, the following inequality holds:

\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{(S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k})}}{B_{k}}-(\mathbf{p}^{a}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}^{a}_{K}),\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\nabla F\left(z*(\mathbf{p}^{a}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}^{a}_{K})\right)\mathrm{d}z\right\rangle\leq 0,

where
w(z)
is the corresponding weight function.

then, following the part
1)

Lemma

1
, we can show that when
f
is monotone
\alpha
-weakly DR-submodular and
w(z)=e^{\alpha(z-1)}
, for any stationary point
(\mathbf{p}^{a}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}^{a}_{K})\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{K}}
,

F(\mathbf{p}^{a}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}^{a}_{K})\geq\big(1-e^{-\alpha}\big)f(S),

where
S
is any subset within the constraint of problem (
1
).

So we get the result of part
1)
of
Theorem

4
. Similarly, from
2)
of
Lemma

1
, we also can achieve the part
2)
of
Theorem

4
, i.e.,

F(\mathbf{p}^{a}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}^{a}_{K})\geq\big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\big)f(S^{*}),

where
S^{*}
is the optimal solution of problem (
1
).

Appendix E
Rounding Without Replacement

E-A

Proof of
Theorem

5

In this subsection, we aim to prove
Theorem

5
.

At first, due to the normalization in Lines 2-6 of
Algorithm

1
, we assume that each
\|\mathbf{p}_{k}\|_{1}=1,\forall k\in[K]
in this subsection. Subsequently, under this assumption, we show that the subset obtained from the sampling-without-replacement operation, i.e., Lines 7-8 of
Algorithm

1
,
is
lossless
in expectation. Before that, we provide a detailed description of the sampling-without-replacement process of Lines 7-8 of
Algorithm

1
, as shown in
Algorithm

5
.

Algorithm 5

Sampling Without Replacement

Input:
Point
(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
where
\mathbf{p}_{k}=(p_{k}^{1},\dots,p_{k}^{n_{k}})
and
\sum_{m=1}^{n_{k}}p_{k}^{m}=1
for any
k\in[K]
, Partition
(\mathcal{V}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{V}_{K})
of set
\mathcal{V}
where
\mathcal{V}_{k}\triangleq\{v_{k}^{1},\dots,v_{k}^{n_{k}}\}
for any
k\in[K]
, Budget set
\{B_{1},\dots,B_{K}\}

1:

Initialize

S=\emptyset
;

2:

for

\hat{k}=1,\dots,K

do

3:

Compute
B^{\hat{k}}_{min}\triangleq\min(\|\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}\|_{0},B_{\hat{k}})
;

4:

Set
P=0
;

5:

for

\hat{b}=1,\dots,B^{\hat{k}}_{min}

do

6:

if

\hat{b}=1

then

7:

Sampling a number
N_{1}
from
[n_{k}]
according to the probability Pr
(N_{1}=m)=p_{\hat{k}}^{m}
for any
m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]
;

8:

else

9:

Sampling a number
N_{\hat{b}}
from
[n_{k}]-\{N_{1},\dots,N_{\hat{b}-1}\}
according to the probability
\text{Pr}(N_{\hat{b}}=m)\triangleq\frac{p_{\hat{k}}^{m}}{1-P}
for any
m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]\setminus\{N_{1},\dots,N_{\hat{b}-1}\}
;

10:

end

if

11:

end

for

12:

Compute
\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}\triangleq\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{min}^{\hat{k}}}\{v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}
;

13:

end

for

14:

Return

S\triangleq\cup_{\hat{k}=1}^{K}\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}
;

The core of Algorithm
5
lies in the Line 9, that is, instead of independently sampling according to each probability vector
\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}
, we take into account the elements previously selected within the same community, namely,
\{v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{1}},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}-1}}\}
. Next, we show the sampling-without-replacement process of
Algorithm

5
is
lossless
with respect to the function value of our introduced multinoulli extension in expectation, that is to say,

Lemma 2
.

For any multinoulli priors
(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
satisfying
\|\mathbf{p}_{k}\|_{1}=1,\forall k\in[K]
, if the set function
f
is monotone, we can show that the subset
S
returned by
Algorithm

5
satisfies:

•

|S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}|\equiv B^{k}_{min}
where
B^{k}_{min}\triangleq\min(\|\mathbf{p}_{k}\|_{0},B_{k})
for any
k\in[K]
;

•

E\Big(f(S)\Big)\geq F(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})
where
F
is the Multinoulli Extension of
f
.

Proof.

The first point of
Lemma

2
is easy to verify. We mainly focus on the second point.

At first, fixing a
\hat{k}\in[K]
and any subset
\tilde{S}\subseteq\mathcal{V}
, we show the following inequality holds:

\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}\}\Big)\Bigg)\geq\mathbb{E}_{e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}\sim\text{Multi}(\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}),\forall\hat{b}\in[B_{\hat{k}}]}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}}e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}\}\Big)\Bigg),

(31)

where the symbol
e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}\sim\text{Multi}(\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}),\forall\hat{b}\in[B_{\hat{k}}]
implies that each element
e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}
is independently drawn from
\mathcal{V}_{\hat{k}}
according to the distribution
\text{Multi}(\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}})
.

Note that, when
\|\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}\|_{0}<B_{\hat{k}}
, from Lines 6-10 of
Algorithm

5
, we can know that the set
\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}
contains all the elements with nonzero probabilities in vector
\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}
. As a result, for any element
e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}\sim\text{Multi}(\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}),\forall\hat{b}\in[B_{\hat{k}}]
, we have
e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}\in\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}
such that
\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\{e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}\}\subseteq\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}
. Naturally, from the monotonicity of
f
, we get the result of Eq.(
31
). Next, we investigate the case where
B^{\hat{k}}_{min}\triangleq\min(\|\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}\|_{0},B_{\hat{k}})=B_{\hat{k}}
. In this scenario, in order to prove the result of Eq.(
31
). We first show that

\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)\geq\mathbb{E}_{e_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\sim\text{Multi}(\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}})}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{e_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

(32)

Next, we show the verification of Eq.(
32
), that is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)=\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]\setminus\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}}\frac{p_{\hat{k}}^{m}}{1-P}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{m}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{P}{1-P}\sum_{m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]\setminus\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}}p_{\hat{k}}^{m}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{m}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]\setminus\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}}p_{\hat{k}}^{m}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{m}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{P}{1-P}\left(\sum_{m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]\setminus\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}}p_{\hat{k}}^{m}\right)\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]\setminus\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(p_{\hat{k}}^{m}f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{m}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg(Pf\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)+\sum_{m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]\setminus\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}}p_{\hat{k}}^{m}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{m}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{m\in\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(p_{\hat{k}}^{m}f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]\setminus\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}}p_{\hat{k}}^{m}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{m}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{m\in\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(p_{\hat{k}}^{m}f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{m}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]\setminus\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}}p_{\hat{k}}^{m}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{m}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{m\in[n_{\hat{k}}]}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(p_{\hat{k}}^{m}f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{m}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{e_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\sim\text{Multi}(\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}})}\Bigg(f\Big(\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{e_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\Bigg),

where the first equality follows from
B^{\hat{k}}_{min}=B_{\hat{k}}
, the second equality comes from
P\triangleq\sum_{m\in\{N_{1},\dots,N_{N_{B_{\hat{k}}}-1}\}}p_{\hat{k}}^{m}
, the first inequality comes from the monotonicity of
f
, the fifth equality follows from
P\triangleq\sum_{m\in\{N_{1},\dots,N_{N_{B_{\hat{k}}}-1}\}}p_{\hat{k}}^{m}
, the sixth equality comes from
\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{m}\}=\tilde{S}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\}
when
m\in\{N_{1},\dots,N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}
and the final equality follows from that the element
e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}
is randomly chosen according to the distribution
\text{Multi}(\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}})
and
\|\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}\|_{1}=1
.

With Eq.(
32
), we then fix a random element
e_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\in\mathcal{V}_{\hat{k}}\cup\{\emptyset\}
and re-use the result of Eq.(
32
) to the new objective
\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\big(\tilde{S}\cup\{e_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\}\big)\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-2}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-2}\Bigg)
such that we can have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg(f\Big(\big(\tilde{S}\cup\{e_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\}\big)\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-2}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{B_{\hat{k}}-1}}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}_{e_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\sim\text{Multi}(\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}})}\Bigg(f\Big(\big(\tilde{S}\cup\{e_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\}\big)\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}-2}v_{\hat{k}}^{N_{\hat{b}}}\}\cup\{e_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-1}\}\Big)\Big|v_{\hat{k}}^{1},\dots,v_{\hat{k}}^{B_{\hat{k}}-2}\Bigg).

By repeatedly iterating the process described above, we will eventually obtain the result of Eq.(
31
) for the case where
B^{\hat{k}}_{min}\triangleq\min(\|\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}\|_{0},B_{\hat{k}})=B_{\hat{k}}
.

With the result of Eq.(
31
), we then can show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{S}\left(f\left(S\right)\right)=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}},\hat{k}\in[K]}\left(f\left(\cup_{\hat{k}=1}^{K}\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{A}_{K}}\left(f\big(\{\cup_{\hat{k}=1}^{K-1}\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}\}\cup\{\mathcal{A}_{K}\}\big)\Big|\mathcal{A}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{A}_{K-1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbb{E}_{e_{K}^{\hat{b}}\sim\text{Multi}(\mathbf{p}_{K}),\forall\hat{b}\in[B_{K}]}\left(f\big(\{\cup_{\hat{k}=1}^{K-1}\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}\}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{K}}e_{K}^{\hat{b}}\}\big)\Big|\mathcal{A}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{A}_{K-1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg(\mathbb{E}\left(f\big(\big\{\{\cup_{\hat{k}=1}^{K-2}\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}\}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{K}}e_{K}^{\hat{b}}\}\big\}\cup\{\mathcal{A}_{K-1}\}\big)\Big|\mathcal{A}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{A}_{K-2},\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{K}}e_{K}^{\hat{b}}\}\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\Bigg(\mathbb{E}_{e_{K-1}^{\hat{b}}\sim\text{Multi}(\mathbf{p}_{K}),\forall\hat{b}\in[B_{K-1}]}\left(f\Big(\{\cup_{\hat{k}=1}^{K-2}\mathcal{A}_{\hat{k}}\}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{K}}e_{K}^{\hat{b}}\}\cup\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{K-1}}e_{K-1}^{\hat{b}}\}\Big)\Big|\mathcal{A}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{A}_{K-2},\{\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{K}}e_{K}^{\hat{b}}\}\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\dots

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}_{e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}\sim\text{Multi($\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}$)},\forall\hat{k}\in[K],\hat{b}\in[B_{\hat{k}}]}\Big(f\big(\cup_{\hat{k}=1}^{K}\cup_{\hat{b}=1}^{B_{\hat{k}}}\{e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}\}\big)\Big)=F(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}).

Thus, we get the
Lemma

2
.
∎

With the result of
Lemma

2
, we prove the
Theorem

5
for our
Algorithm

1
.

Proof.

At first, when
\|\mathbf{p}_{k}\|_{1}>0
, we define
\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{k}\triangleq\frac{\mathbf{p}_{k}}{\|\mathbf{p}_{k}\|_{1}}
. Otherwise, we set
\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{k}\triangleq(\frac{1}{n_{k}},\dots,\frac{1}{n_{k}})\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{k}}
. From the result of
Lemma

2
, we can have
\mathbb{E}\Big(f\Big(\cup_{k=1}^{K}\cup_{b=1}^{B_{min}^{k}}\{e_{k}^{b}\}\Big)\Big)\geq F(\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{1},\dots,\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{K})
. Next, according to the monotonicity of
f
and
B_{min}^{k}\leq B_{k}
, we also can show that
\mathbb{E}\Big(f\Big(\cup_{k=1}^{K}\cup_{b=1}^{B_{k}}\{e_{k}^{b}\}\Big)\Big)\geq\mathbb{E}\Big(f\Big(\cup_{k=1}^{K}\cup_{b=1}^{B_{min}^{k}}\{e_{k}^{b}\}\Big)\Big)
such that
\mathbb{E}\big(f(S)\big)=\mathbb{E}\Big(f\Big(\cup_{k=1}^{K}\cup_{b=1}^{B_{k}}\{e_{k}^{b}\}\Big)\Big)\geq F(\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{1},\dots,\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{K})
. Furthermore, from the second point of
Theorem

1
, we know that when the set function
f
exhibits monotonicity, its Multinoulli Extension
F
is also monotone. As a result,
F(\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{1},\dots,\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{K})\geq F(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})
where the definition of
\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{k}
implies that
\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{k}\geq\mathbf{p}_{k}
for any
k\in[K]
.

From the previous discussions, we can infer that
\mathbb{E}\big(f(S)\big)=\mathbb{E}\Big(f\Big(\cup_{k=1}^{K}\cup_{b=1}^{B_{k}}\{e_{k}^{b}\}\Big)\Big)\geq F(\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{1},\dots,\tilde{\mathbf{p}}_{K})\geq F(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})
. Thus, we prove the second point in
Theorem

5
. Similarly, from the first point of
Lemma

2
and the Lines 9-11 in
Algorithm

1
, i.e., the complement sampling process, we can easily show the first point of
Theorem

5
.

∎

Appendix F
Continuous Greedy Method for Multinoulli Extension

F-A

Proof of
Theorem

7

In this section, we prove the Theorem
7
.

Before going into the details, we firstly bound each second-order derivative of our proposed
ME

F
, that is to say,

Lemma 3
.

Given a monotone set function
f:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, if we denote the maximum marginal value of
f
as
M_{f}=\max_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},e\in\mathcal{V}\setminus S}\Big(f(e|S)\Big)
, we have that, for any
k_{1},k_{2}\in[K]
,
m_{1}\in[n_{k_{1}}]
and
m_{2}\in[n_{k_{2}}]
,

\Big|\frac{\partial^{2}F}{\partial p_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}\partial p_{k_{2}}^{m_{2}}}(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\Big|\leq\bar{B}^{2}M_{f},

where
(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and
\bar{B}=\max_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
is the maximum budget over the K communities
\{\mathcal{V}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{V}_{K}\}
.

Proof.

From the Theorem
6
, we know that, If
k_{1}\neq k_{2}\in[K]
, the second-order derivative of the Multinoulli Extension
F
at any point
(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
can be written as follows:

\frac{\partial^{2}F}{\partial p_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}\partial p_{k_{2}}^{m_{2}}}(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})=B_{k_{1}}B_{k_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}}\Big(f\big(v_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}|S\cup\{v_{k_{2}}^{m_{2}}\}\big)-f\big(v_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}|S\big)\Big),

where
S=\cup_{(\hat{k},\hat{b})\neq\left\{(k_{1},1),(k_{2},1)\right\}}\{e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}\}
and each
e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}
is drawn from the multinoulli distribution Multi(
\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}
). Furthermore, for any monotone set function
f
and any subset
S\subseteq\mathcal{V}
, we can easily know that:

-M_{f}\leq-f\big(v_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}|S\big)\leq f\big(v_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}|S\cup\{v_{k_{2}}^{m_{2}}\}\big)-f\big(v_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}|S\big)\leq f\big(v_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}|S\cup\{v_{k_{2}}^{m_{2}}\}\big)\leq M_{f}

such that, when
k_{1}\neq k_{2}\in[K]
,

|\frac{\partial^{2}F}{\partial p_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}\partial p_{k_{2}}^{m_{2}}}(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})|\leq B_{k_{1}}B_{k_{2}}M_{f}\leq\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}.

Similarly, we also can verify that when
k_{1}=k_{2}=k\in[K]
, for any
m_{1}\in[n_{k_{1}}]
and
m_{2}\in[n_{k_{2}}]
, we have that
\Big|\frac{\partial^{2}F}{\partial p_{k}^{m_{1}}\partial p_{k}^{m_{2}}}(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\Big|\leq\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}
.
∎

Similarly, we also can verify that the estimations of the second-order derivative of Multinoulli Extension
F
in Remark
8
are also bounded by
\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}
, that is to say,

Lemma 4
.

Given a monotone set function
f:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, if we denote the maximum marginal value of
f
as
M_{f}=\max_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},e\in\mathcal{V}\setminus S}\Big(f(e|S)\Big)
, we can infer that each second-order estimators in Remark
8
is also bounded by
\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}
, i.e., for any
k_{1},k_{2}\in[K]
,
m_{1}\in[n_{k_{1}}]
and
m_{2}\in[n_{k_{2}}]
, we have that

|\widehat{\frac{\partial^{2}F}{\partial p_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}\partial p_{k_{2}}^{m_{2}}}}((\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K}))|\leq\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}

where
(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and
\bar{B}=\max_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
is the maximum budget over the K communities
\{\mathcal{V}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{V}_{K}\}
.

As a result, we also show that

Lemma 5
.

Given a monotone set function
f:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, if we denote the maximum marginal value of
f
as
M_{f}=\max_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},e\in\mathcal{V}\setminus S}\Big(f(e|S)\Big)
, we can infer that each Hessian approximation
\widehat{\nabla}^{2}_{t}:=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\widehat{\nabla}^{2}F\big(\mathbf{x}_{l}(t)\big)
in Line 9 of Algorithm
2
satisfies that,

\|\widehat{\nabla}^{2}_{t}\|^{2}_{2,\infty}\leq n\bar{B}^{4}M^{2}_{f}

where
t\in[T]
,
n=|\mathcal{V}|
,
L
is a positive integer,
\bar{B}=\max_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
is the maximum budget over the K communities
\{\mathcal{V}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{V}_{K}\}
.

Remark 19
.

For any matrix
A\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
, the
(2,\infty)
-norm of
A
is defined as
\|A\|_{2,\infty}=\sup\{\|A\mathbf{x}\|_{\infty}:\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{n},\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}=1\}
where
\|\cdot\|
denotes the L2 norm.

Proof.

From the definition of the norm
\|\cdot\|_{2,\infty}
, we can show that

\|\widehat{\nabla}^{2}_{t}\|^{2}_{2,\infty}=\max_{i\in[n]}\|\widehat{\nabla}^{2}_{t}(i,:)\|_{2}^{2}\leq n\bar{B}^{4}M^{2}_{f},

where
\widehat{\nabla}^{2}_{t}(i,:)
is the
i
-th line of the Hessian approximation
\widehat{\nabla}^{2}_{t}
and the final inequality follows from the
Lemma

4
.
∎

With the
Lemma

5
, we next verify the gap between our gradient estimator
\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t)
and the exact gradient
\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))
, that is,

Lemma 6
.

Given a monotone set function
f:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, if we denote the maximum marginal value of
f
as
M_{f}=\max_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},e\in\mathcal{V}\setminus S}\Big(f(e|S)\Big)
, we can show that each gradient estimator
\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t)
in Line 11 of Algorithm
2
satisfies that, for any
t\in[T]

\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)\leq\frac{nr\bar{B}^{4}M^{2}_{f}}{LT},

where
L
is the batch size,
n=|\mathcal{V}|
,
\bar{B}=\max_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
is the maximum budget over the K communities
\{\mathcal{V}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{V}_{K}\}
and the rank
r=\sum_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
.

Proof.

Note that in Lines 4 of Algorithm
2
, we compute the exact gradient of our proposed Multinoulli Extension
F
at the point
0
and then assign this value to
\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(1)
. Therefore, we know that when
t=1
,

\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(1))\|=\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(1)-\nabla F(\textbf{0})\|=0\leq\frac{nr\bar{B}^{4}M^{2}_{f}}{LT}.

When
t>1
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t-1)+\boldsymbol{\xi}_{t}-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)+\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}_{t}-\Big(\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\Big)\|^{2}_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\boldsymbol{g}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1)),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{t}-\Big(\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\Big)\right\rangle\Big).

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\boldsymbol{g}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1)),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{t}-\Big(\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\Big)\right\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg(\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\boldsymbol{g}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1)),\boldsymbol{\xi}_{t}-\Big(\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\Big)\right\rangle\Big|\mathbf{x}(t)\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\boldsymbol{g}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1)),\mathbb{E}\Big(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{t}|\mathbf{x}(t)\Big)-\Big(\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\Big)\right\rangle\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle=0.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)+\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}_{t}-\Big(\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\Big)\|^{2}_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)+\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\|\widehat{\nabla}^{2}_{t}\big(\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(t-1)\big)-\Big(\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\Big)\right\|^{2}_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)+\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\|\Big(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\Big(\widehat{\nabla}^{2}F\big(\mathbf{x}_{l}(t)\big)\big(\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(t-1)\big)\Big)-\Big(\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\Big)\right\|^{2}_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(\left\|\Big(\widehat{\nabla}^{2}F\big(\mathbf{x}_{1}(t)\big)\big(\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(t-1)\big)\Big)-\Big(\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\Big)\right\|^{2}_{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(\|\widehat{\nabla}^{2}F\big(\mathbf{x}_{1}(t)\big)\big(\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(t-1)\big)\|^{2}_{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(\|\widehat{\nabla}^{2}F\big(\mathbf{x}_{1}(t)\big)\|^{2}_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(t-1)\|^{2}_{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\Bigg(\|\widehat{\nabla}^{2}F\big(\mathbf{x}_{1}(t)\big)\|^{2}_{2,\infty}\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t-1)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\|^{2}_{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(t-1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t-1))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)+\frac{nr\bar{B}^{4}M^{2}_{f}}{LT^{2}}

\displaystyle\dots

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}(1)-\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(1))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)+\frac{nr\bar{B}^{4}M^{2}_{f}}{LT^{2}}(t-1)\leq\frac{nr\bar{B}^{4}M^{2}_{f}}{LT},

where the first inequality follows from
\mathbb{E}\big(X-\mathbb{E}(X)\big)^{2}\leq\mathbb{E}(X^{2})
for any random variable
X
; the second inequality comes from the definition of the norm
\|\cdot\|_{2,\infty}
; the third inequality follows from the
Lemma

5
and the ascent direction
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t-1)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}
has at most
r
non-zero elements.
∎

Now, we verify the Theorem
7
.

Proof.

From calculus, we know that, there exist a constant
a\in[0,1]
such that

F(\mathbf{x}(t+1))-F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t)),\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\right\rangle=\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\nabla^{2}F(\mathbf{x}^{a}(t+1))\Big(\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\Big),\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\right\rangle,

where
\mathbf{x}^{a}(t+1)=a\mathbf{x}(t+1)+(1-a)\mathbf{x}(t)
.
Therefore, we can show that

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}(t+1))

(33)

\displaystyle\geq F(\mathbf{x}(t))+\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t)),\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\right\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\left|\left\langle\nabla^{2}F(\mathbf{x}^{a}(t+1))\Big(\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\Big),\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\geq F(\mathbf{x}(t))+\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t)),\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\right\rangle-\frac{\|\nabla^{2}F(\mathbf{x}^{a}(t+1))\|_{2,\infty}}{2}\|\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq F(\mathbf{x}(t))+\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t)),\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\right\rangle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{2}\|\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\|_{2}^{2},

where the final inequality follows from
Lemma

5
.

With Eq.(
33
) and
\mathbf{x}(t+1)=\mathbf{x}(t)+\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{v}(t)
, we also have that,for any subset
S
within the partition constraint of problem (
1
),

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}(t+1))

\displaystyle\geq F(\mathbf{x}(t))+\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t)),\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\right\rangle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{2}\|\mathbf{x}(t+1)-\mathbf{x}(t)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=F(\mathbf{x}(t))+\frac{1}{T}\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t)),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\right\rangle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{2T^{2}}\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=F(\mathbf{x}(t))+\frac{1}{T}\left\langle\boldsymbol{g}(t),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{T}\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\boldsymbol{g}(t),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\right\rangle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{2T^{2}}\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq F(\mathbf{x}(t))+\frac{1}{T}\left\langle\boldsymbol{g}(t),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{T}\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\boldsymbol{g}(t),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\right\rangle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{2T^{2}}\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\|_{2}^{2},

where the final inequality follows from
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{T}\Delta_{n_{k}}
if the subset
S
is included into the partition constraint of problem (
1
) and Line 13 in
Algorithm

2
.

As a result, we can show that, in expectation,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t+1))\big)

(34)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)+\frac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\big(\left\langle\boldsymbol{g}(t),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\big)+\frac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\big(\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\boldsymbol{g}(t),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\right\rangle\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{2T^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big(\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)+\frac{1}{T}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\Big(\boldsymbol{g}(t)\Big),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\big(\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\boldsymbol{g}(t),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\right\rangle\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{2T^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big(\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)+\frac{1}{T}\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t)),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\big(\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\boldsymbol{g}(t),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\right\rangle\big)

\displaystyle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{2T^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big(\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\big),

where the final equality follows from
\boldsymbol{g}(\mathbf{x}(t))
is the unbiased estimator of
\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))
for any
t\in[T]
.

1):
When the set function
f:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is monotone and
\alpha
-weakly DR-submodular, from
Theorem

2
, we can show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t+1))\big)

(35)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)+\frac{1}{T}\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t)),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\big(\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\boldsymbol{g}(t),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\big)-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{2T^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big(\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)+\frac{\alpha}{T}\Big(f(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\Big)++\frac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\big(\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\boldsymbol{g}(t),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\big)-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{2T^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big(\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)+\frac{\alpha}{T}\Big(f(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\Big)-\frac{1}{2\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}\Big(\|F(\mathbf{x}(t))-\boldsymbol{g}(t)\|_{2}^{2}\Big)-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}\sqrt{n}}{T^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big(\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{B_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{S(t)\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\big)\big)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)+\frac{\alpha}{T}\Big(f(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)\Big)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2LT}-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T^{2}}

where the second inequality follows from the Young’s inequality and the final inequality comes from
Lemma

6
.

By rearranging the Eq.(
35
), we can show that

\displaystyle\Big(f(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t+1))\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\alpha}{T})\Big(f(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\Big)+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2LT}+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\dots

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\alpha}{T})^{t}\Big(f(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(1))\Big)+\Big(\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2LT}+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T^{2}}\Big)\sum_{i=0}^{t-1}(1-\frac{\alpha}{T})^{i}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\alpha}{T})^{t}\Big(f(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(1))\Big)+\Big(\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2LT}+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T^{2}}\Big)\frac{T}{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\alpha}{T})^{t}f(S)+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2\alpha L}+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{\alpha T}.

Finally, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(T+1)\big)

\displaystyle\geq\Big(1-(1-\frac{\alpha}{T})^{T}\Big)f(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2\alpha L}-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{\alpha T}

\displaystyle\geq\Big(1-e^{-\alpha}\Big)f(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2\alpha L}-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{\alpha T},

where the final inequality follows from
(1-\frac{\alpha}{T})^{T}\leq e^{-\alpha}
when
T\geq 3
.

Therefore, when
L=\frac{T}{2}
, we have that

\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(T+1)\big)\geq\Big(1-e^{-\alpha}\Big)f(S^{*})-\frac{2r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{\alpha T}.

2:
When the set function
f:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is monotone and
(\gamma,\beta)
-weakly submodular, from the Theorem
2
, we can show that

\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t+1))\big)\geq\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)+\frac{\gamma^{2}}{T}f(S)-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{T}\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2LT}-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T^{2}}.

(36)

By rearranging the Eq.(
36
), we can have that

\displaystyle\Big(\gamma^{2}f(S)-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t+1))\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{T})\Big(\gamma^{2}f(S)-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(t))\big)\Big)+\Big(\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2LT}+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T^{2}}\Big)(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})

\displaystyle\leq\dots

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{T})^{t}\Big(f\gamma^{2}f(S)-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(0))\Big)+\Big(\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2LT}+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T^{2}}\Big)T

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{T})^{t}\gamma^{2}f(S)+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2L}+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T}.

Finally, we have that

\displaystyle(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(T+1)\big)

\displaystyle\geq\Big(1-(1-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{T})^{T}\Big)\gamma^{2}f(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2L}+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T}

\displaystyle\geq\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})})f(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{2L}+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T},

where the final inequality follows from
(1-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{T})^{T}\leq e^{-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})}
when
T\geq 3
.

Therefore, when
L=\frac{T}{2}
, we have that

\mathbb{E}\big(F(\mathbf{x}(T+1)\big)\geq\Big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-(\gamma(1-\beta)+\gamma^{2})})}{\gamma(1-\beta)+\gamma^{2}}\Big)f(S)-\frac{2r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{f}}{T(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})}.

∎

Remark 20
.

If
T=L=\mathcal{O}(\frac{r\sqrt{n}}{\epsilon})
and
S
is the optimal subset of problem (
1
), we can show that, when the objective function is monotone
\alpha
-weakly DR-submodular or
(\gamma,\beta)
-weakly submodular, our
Multinoulli-SCG
algorithm can attain a value of
(1-e^{-\alpha})f(S)-\epsilon
or
(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})})}{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}})f(S)-\epsilon
. Note that, during the process of
\mathcal{O}(\frac{r\sqrt{n}}{\epsilon})
iterations, if
L=\mathcal{O}(\frac{r\sqrt{n}}{\epsilon})
, due to
Remark

11
,
Multinoulli-SCG
only requires evaluating the set function
\mathcal{O}(\frac{r^{3}n^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})
times.

F-B

Proof of
Theorem

8

This subsection aims to provide a proof for
Theorem

8
. At first, like
Lemma

3
,
Lemma

5
and
Lemma

6
, if we denote the maximum marginal value of the sequence
\{f_{1},\dots,f_{T}\}
as
M_{1:T}\triangleq\max_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},e\in\mathcal{V}\setminus S,t\in[T]}\Big(f_{t}(e|S)\Big)
, then we can show that

Lemma 7
.

Given a sequence of monotone set functions
\{f_{1},\dots,f_{T}\}
where
f_{t}:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, if we denote the maximum marginal value of
\{f_{1},\dots,f_{T}\}
as
M_{1:T}\triangleq\max_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},e\in\mathcal{V}\setminus S,t\in[T]}\Big(f_{t}(e|S)\Big)
, we have that, for any
k_{1},k_{2}\in[K]
,
m_{1}\in[n_{k_{1}}]
and
m_{2}\in[n_{k_{2}}]
,

\Big|\frac{\partial^{2}F_{t}}{\partial p_{k_{1}}^{m_{1}}\partial p_{k_{2}}^{m_{2}}}(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\Big|\leq\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T},

where
F_{t}
is the
ME
of
f_{t}
for any
t\in[T]
,
(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and
\bar{B}=\max_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
is the maximum budget over the K communities
\{\mathcal{V}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{V}_{K}\}
.

Lemma 8
.

Given a sequence of monotone set functions
\{f_{1},\dots,f_{T}\}
where
f_{t}:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, if we denote the maximum marginal value of
\{f_{1},\dots,f_{T}\}
as
M_{1:T}\triangleq\max_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},e\in\mathcal{V}\setminus S,t\in[T]}\Big(f_{t}(e|S)\Big)
, we can infer that each Hessian approximation
\widehat{\nabla}^{2}_{t}(q)\triangleq\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l=1}^{L}\widehat{\nabla}^{2}F_{t}\big(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{(l)}(q)\big),\forall q\in[Q],\forall t\in[T]
in Line 17 of Algorithm
3
satisfies that,

\|\widehat{\nabla}^{2}_{t}(q)\|^{2}_{2,\infty}\leq n\bar{B}^{4}M_{1:T}^{2}

where
t\in[T]
,
n=|\mathcal{V}|
,
L
is a positive integer,
\bar{B}=\max_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
is the maximum budget over the
K
communities
\{\mathcal{V}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{V}_{K}\}
.

Lemma 9
.

Given a sequence of monotone set functions
\{f_{1},\dots,f_{T}\}
where
f_{t}:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, if we denote the maximum marginal value of
\{f_{1},\dots,f_{T}\}
as
M_{1:T}\triangleq\max_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},e\in\mathcal{V}\setminus S,t\in[T]}\Big(f_{t}(e|S)\Big)
, we can show that each gradient estimator
\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\forall q\in[Q],\forall t\in[T]
in Line 19 of Algorithm
3
satisfies that, for any
t\in[T]

\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q)-\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))\|^{2}_{2}\Big)\leq\frac{nr\bar{B}^{4}M^{2}_{1:T}}{LQ},

where
L
is the batch size,
n=|\mathcal{V}|
,
\bar{B}=\max_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
is the maximum budget over the
K
communities
\{\mathcal{V}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{V}_{K}\}
and the rank
r=\sum_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
.

Now, we verify the Theorem
8
.

Proof.

From calculus, we know that, there exist a constant
a\in[0,1]
such that

F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))-F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))-\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)),\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)-\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)\right\rangle=\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\nabla^{2}F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{a}(q+1)))\Big(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)-\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)\Big),\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)-\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)\right\rangle,

where
\mathbf{x}_{t}^{a}(q+1)=a\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)+(1-a)\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)
.
Therefore, we can show that

\displaystyle F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))

(37)

\displaystyle\geq F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))+\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)),\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)-\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)\right\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\left|\left\langle\nabla^{2}F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{a}_{t}(q+1))\Big(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)-\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)\Big),\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)-\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\geq F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))+\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)),\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)-\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)\right\rangle-\frac{\|\nabla^{2}F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{a}_{t}(q+1))\|_{2,\infty}}{2}\|\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)-\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))+\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)),\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)-\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)\right\rangle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}\sqrt{n}}{2}\|\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)-\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)\|_{2}^{2},

where the final inequality follows from
Lemma

8
.

Next, according to the Line 6 of
Algorithm

3
, i.e.,
\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1)\triangleq\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)+\frac{1}{Q}\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)
, we also can show that, for any subset
S
within the partition constraint of problem (
1
),

\displaystyle F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))

\displaystyle\geq F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))+\frac{1}{Q}\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}\sqrt{n}}{2Q^{2}}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))+\frac{1}{Q}\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{Q}\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{Q}\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))-\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}\sqrt{n}}{2Q^{2}}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\|_{2}^{2}.

It is worth noting that when
\mathbf{x}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
, we have
\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{x}\|_{1}\leq K\leq r
where the rank
r\triangleq\sum_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
. As a result, we can have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\Big|\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))-\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\big|\right)+\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}\sqrt{n}}{2Q^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(\|\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\|_{2}^{2}\right)

(38)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}\left(\|\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))-\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q)\|_{2}^{2}\right)+\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}\sqrt{n}}{2Q^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(\|\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}\sqrt{n}}{2Q^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left(\|\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2LQ}+\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q^{2}},

where the first inequality follows from the Young’s inequality and the second inequality comes from
Lemma

9
and the truth that
\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
such that
\mathbb{E}(\|\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\|_{2}^{2})\leq r
and
\mathbb{E}(\|\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2})\leq\mathbb{E}(\|\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\|_{2}^{2})+\mathbb{E}(\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2})\leq 2r
.

With the result of Eq.(
38
), we can have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))\right)\geq\mathbb{E}\left(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))+\frac{1}{Q}\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{Q}\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{Q}\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))-\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle-\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}\sqrt{n}}{2Q^{2}}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\left(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))+\frac{1}{Q}\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{Q}\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2LQ}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q^{2}}.

1):
When each set function
f_{t}:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is monotone and
\alpha
-weakly DR-submodular, from
Theorem

2
, we can show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))\right)

(39)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\left(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))\right)+\frac{1}{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q)),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\right)+\frac{1}{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2LQ}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\Big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))\Big)+\frac{\alpha}{Q}\Big(f_{t}(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))\big)\Big)+\frac{1}{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2LQ}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q^{2}}.

By rearranging the Eq.(
39
), we can show that

\displaystyle f_{t}(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))\big)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\alpha}{Q})\left(f_{t}(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))\big)\right)+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2LQ}+\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q^{2}}+\frac{1}{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq\dots

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\alpha}{Q})^{q}\left(f_{t}(S)-\mathbb{E}\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(1))\big)\right)+\Big(\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2LQ}+\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q^{2}}\Big)\sum_{i=0}^{q-1}(1-\frac{\alpha}{Q})^{i}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{i=1}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(i),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(i)\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\frac{\alpha}{Q})^{q}f_{t}(S)+\Big(\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2LQ}+\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q^{2}}\Big)\sum_{i=0}^{q-1}(1-\frac{\alpha}{Q})^{i}+\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{i=1}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(i),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(i)\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\alpha}{Q})^{q}f_{t}(S)+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2L\alpha}+\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha Q}+\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{i=1}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(i),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(i)\right\rangle\right),

where the final equality follows from
\mathbf{x}_{t}(1)\triangleq\boldsymbol{0}_{n},F_{t}(\boldsymbol{0}_{n})=0
and the final inequality comes from
\sum_{i=0}^{q-1}(1-\frac{\alpha}{Q})^{i}\leq\frac{Q}{\alpha}
.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle E\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(Q+1))\big)

(40)

\displaystyle\geq\left(1-(1-\frac{\alpha}{Q})^{Q}\right)f_{t}(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2L\alpha}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha Q}-\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{q=1}^{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq\left(1-e^{-\alpha}\right)f_{t}(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2L\alpha}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha Q}-\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{q=1}^{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle\right),

where the second inequality comes from
(1-\frac{\alpha}{Q})^{Q}\leq e^{-\alpha}
.

Then, summing up both sides of Eq.(
40
) from
t=1,\dots,T
, we can get

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(Q+1))\big)

\displaystyle\geq\left(1-e^{-\alpha}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha}\frac{T}{L}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha}\frac{T}{Q}-\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q=1}^{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1-e^{-\alpha}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha}\frac{T}{L}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha}\frac{T}{Q}-\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{q=1}^{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq\left(1-e^{-\alpha}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha}\frac{T}{L}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha}\frac{T}{Q}-\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{q=1}^{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\text{Reg}_{1}^{\mathcal{E}^{(q)}}(T)\right),

where the final inequality comes from the definition of
1-Regret
of each linear maximization oracle
\mathcal{E}^{(q)},\forall q\in[Q]
, that is,
\text{Reg}_{1}^{\mathcal{E}^{(q)}}(T)\triangleq\max_{\mathbf{x}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{x}(q)\rangle-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\rangle
. Note that, when
S
within the partition constraint of problem (
1
),
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
such that
\text{Reg}_{1}^{\mathcal{E}^{(q)}}(T)\geq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle
.

Finally, from the
Theorem

5
about the rounding-without-replacement process and Lines 8-9 of
Algorithm

3
, we can infer that the
(1-e^{-\alpha})
-Regret of our proposed
Algorithm

3
, i.e.,
\text{Reg}_{(1-e^{-\alpha})}\left(T\right)
satisfies that

\displaystyle\text{Reg}_{(1-e^{-\alpha})}\left(T\right)\triangleq\left(1-e^{-\alpha}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S^{*})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\big(f_{t}(S_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-e^{-\alpha}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S^{*})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(Q+1))\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha}\frac{T}{L}+\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\alpha}\frac{T}{Q}+\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{q=1}^{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\text{Reg}_{1}^{\mathcal{E}^{(q)}}(T)\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{nr}T^{1/2}+r\sqrt{n}\left(\frac{T}{L}+\frac{T}{Q}\right)\right),

where
S^{*}\triangleq\mathop{\arg\max}_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},|S\cup\mathcal{V}_{k}|\leq B_{k},\forall k\in[K]}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)
and the final equality follows from
1
.

2):
When each set function
f_{t}:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is monotone and
(\gamma,\beta)
-weakly submodular, from the Theorem
2
, we can show

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))\right)\geq\mathbb{E}\Big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))\Big)+\frac{\gamma^{2}}{Q}f_{t}(S)-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{Q}\mathbb{E}\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))\big)\Big)

(41)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\right)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2LQ}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q^{2}}.

By rearranging the Eq.(
41
), we also can have that

\displaystyle\Big(\gamma^{2}f_{t}(S)-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})\mathbb{E}\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q+1))\big)\Big)\leq(1-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{Q})\left(\gamma^{2}f_{t}(S)-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})\mathbb{E}\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(q))\big)\right)

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2LQ}+\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q^{2}}+\frac{1}{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle\right)\Big)(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})

\displaystyle\leq\dots

\displaystyle\leq\gamma^{2}(1-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{Q})^{q}f_{t}(S)+\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2L}+\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q}+\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{Q}\sum_{i=1}^{q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(i),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(i)\right\rangle\right).

As a result, we have that

\displaystyle(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})E\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(Q+1))\big)

(42)

\displaystyle\geq\gamma^{2}\big(1-(1-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{Q})^{Q}\big)f_{t}(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2L}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q}-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{Q}\sum_{q=1}^{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})})f_{t}(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2L}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2Q}-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{Q}\sum_{q=1}^{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle\right),

where the final inequality follows from
(1-\frac{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}{Q})^{Q}\leq e^{-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})}
when
Q\geq 3
.

Then, summing up both sides of Eq.(
42
) from
t=1,\dots,T
, we can get

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(Q+1))\big)

\displaystyle\geq\Big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\Big)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\frac{T}{L}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\frac{T}{Q}-\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q=1}^{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\geq\Big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\Big)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)-\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\frac{T}{L}-\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\frac{T}{Q}-\frac{1}{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\text{Reg}_{1}^{\mathcal{E}^{(q)}}(T)\right),

where
\phi(\gamma,\beta)\triangleq\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}
and the final inequality comes from the definition of
1-Regret
of each linear maximization oracle
\mathcal{E}^{(q)},\forall q\in[Q]
, that is,
\text{Reg}_{1}^{\mathcal{E}^{(q)}}(T)\triangleq\max_{\mathbf{x}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{x}(q)\rangle-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\rangle
. Note that, when
S
within the partition constraint of problem (
1
),
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
such that
\text{Reg}_{1}^{\mathcal{E}^{(q)}}(T)\geq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}(q),\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}-\mathbf{v}_{t}(q)\right\rangle
.

Finally, from the
Theorem

5
about the rounding-without-replacement process and Lines 8-9 of
Algorithm

3
, we can infer that the
\Big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})})}{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}\Big)
-Regret of our proposed
Algorithm

3
satisfies that

\displaystyle\text{Reg}_{\rho}\left(T\right)\triangleq\left(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})}}{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S^{*})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\big(f_{t}(S_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})}}{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S^{*})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}E\big(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}(Q+1))\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})}\frac{T}{L}+\frac{3r\sqrt{n}\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}}{2(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})}\frac{T}{Q}+\frac{1}{Q}\sum_{q=1}^{Q}\mathbb{E}\left(\text{Reg}_{1}^{\mathcal{E}^{(q)}}(T)\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{nr}T^{1/2}+r\sqrt{n}\left(\frac{T}{L}+\frac{T}{Q}\right)\right),

where
\rho\triangleq\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-(\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})})}{\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}
,
S^{*}\triangleq\mathop{\arg\max}_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},|S\cup\mathcal{V}_{k}|\leq B_{k},\forall k\in[K]}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)
and the final equality follows from
1
.
∎

Appendix G
Stochastic Gradient Ascent for Mutinoulli Extension

G-A

Proof of
Theorem

9

In this subsection, we prove the Theorem
9
. At first, we give a lemma about the upper bound of gradients of
F_{t}
where
F_{t}
is the multinoulli extension of
t
-th incoming objective set function
f_{t}
for any
t\in[T]
.

Lemma 10
.

Given a sequence of monotone set functions
\{f_{1},\dots,f_{T}\}
where
f_{t}:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, if we denote the maximum marginal value of
\{f_{1},\dots,f_{T}\}
as
M_{1:T}\triangleq\max_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},e\in\mathcal{V}\setminus S,t\in[T]}\Big(f_{t}(e|S)\Big)
, we have that, for any
k\in[K]
,
m\in[n_{k}]
and
t\in[T]
,

\Big|\frac{\partial F_{t}}{\partial p_{k}^{m}}(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\Big|\leq\bar{B}M_{1:T},

where
(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and
\bar{B}\triangleq\max_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
is the maximum budget over the
K
communities
\{\mathcal{V}_{1},\dots,\mathcal{V}_{K}\}
. Similarly, we also can infer that the gradient estimation in
Remark

3
also can be bounded by
\bar{B}M_{f}
,

\Big|\widehat{\frac{\partial F_{t}}{\partial p_{k}^{m}}}(\mathbf{p}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{p}_{K})\Big|=B_{k}\Big|f_{t}\big(v_{k}^{m}\Big|\cup_{(\hat{k},\hat{b})\neq(k,1)}\{e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}\}\big)\Big|\leq\bar{B}M_{1:T},

where each
e_{\hat{k}}^{\hat{b}}
is independently drawn from the multinoulli distribution ‘Multi(
\mathbf{p}_{\hat{k}}
)’ for any
\hat{k}\in[K]
and
\hat{b}\in[B_{\hat{k}}]
.

With this
Lemma

10
, we then prove the
Theorem

9
.

Proof.

Let
S
denote any subset within the partition constraint of problem (
1
). Then, from the Line 14 in
Algorithm

4
, we know that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}+\eta\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S^{*}\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

(43)

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle+\eta^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2},

where the first inequality follows from the the Line 14 of
Algorithm

4
and
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
due to that
S
is a subset within the partition constraint of problem (
1
).

1)
We consider
Algorithm

4
with the standard gradient estimation(See Lines 6-7). In other words, we choose
\text{Auxiliary}\triangleq\textbf{False}
and set
\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}\triangleq\widehat{\nabla}F_{t}\big(\mathbf{x}_{t}\big)
. As a result, from
Equation

43
, we can have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}(\left\|\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2})

(44)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\widehat{\nabla}F_{t}\big(\mathbf{x}_{t}\big),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}(\left\|\widehat{\nabla}F_{t}\big(\mathbf{x}_{t}\big)\right\|_{2}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\widehat{\nabla}F_{t}\big(\mathbf{x}_{t}\big),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\mathbb{E}\Big(\widehat{\nabla}F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big|\mathbf{x}_{t}\Big),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2},

where the second inequality follows from
Lemma

10
.

Then, if
i):
every set function
f_{t}:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is monotone and
\alpha
-weakly DR-submodular, from
Theorem

11
and Eq.(
44
), we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-2\eta\Big(\alpha f_{t}(S)-(\alpha+\frac{1}{\alpha})F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big)+\eta^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}.

As a result, we have that

\displaystyle 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta\Big(\alpha f_{t}(S)-(\alpha+\frac{1}{\alpha})F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)-E\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)\right)+n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T\eta^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)-E\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{T+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)+n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T\eta^{2}.

Due to that
\mathbb{E}(f_{t}(S_{t}))\geq\mathbb{E}(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))
from
Theorem

5
, we can infer that

\displaystyle\alpha\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)-(\alpha+\frac{1}{\alpha})\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S_{t})\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\alpha f_{t}(S)-(\alpha+\frac{1}{\alpha})F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)}{2\eta}+\frac{n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T}{2}\eta

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\Big(\|\mathbf{x}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\Big)}{2\eta}+\frac{n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T}{2}\eta\leq\frac{r}{\eta}+\frac{n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T}{2}\eta,

where the second inequality follows from
\mathbf{x}_{1}\triangleq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{n_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{V}_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
such that
\left\|\mathbf{x}_{1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{x}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}
and the final inequality comes from the truth that for any
\mathbf{x}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}

\|\mathbf{x}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{x}\|_{1}\leq K\leq r
. Note that
r
is the rank of partition constraint, i.e.,
r=\sum_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}
.

Therefore, we can infer that the
(\frac{\alpha^{2}}{1+\alpha^{2}})
-Regret of our proposed
Algorithm

4
, i.e.,
\text{Reg}_{(\frac{\alpha^{2}}{1+\alpha^{2}})}\left(T\right)
satisfies that

\displaystyle\text{Reg}_{(\frac{\alpha^{2}}{1+\alpha^{2}})}\left(T\right)\triangleq\Big(\frac{\alpha^{2}}{1+\alpha^{2}}\Big)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S^{*})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\alpha}\Big)\Big(\frac{r}{\eta}+\frac{n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T}{2}\eta\Big)\leq\frac{r}{2\eta}+\frac{n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T}{4}\eta=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{r}{\eta}+nT\eta\right)

where
S^{*}\triangleq\mathop{\arg\max}_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},|S\cup\mathcal{V}_{k}|\leq B_{k},\forall k\in[K]}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)
and the final inequality comes from
\frac{1}{\alpha}+\alpha\geq 2
when
\alpha\in(0,1]
.

Furthermore,
ii):
if each set function
f_{t}:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is monotone and
(\gamma,\beta)
-weakly submodular, from
Theorem

11
and Eq.(
44
), we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-2\eta\Big(\gamma^{2}f_{t}(S^{*})-(\beta+\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big)+\eta^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2},

where
S^{*}\triangleq\mathop{\arg\max}_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},|S\cup\mathcal{V}_{k}|\leq B_{k},\forall k\in[K]}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)
.

As a result,

\displaystyle 2\eta\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\gamma^{2}f_{t}(S^{*})-(\beta+\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)-E\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{T+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)+n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T\eta^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2r+n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T\eta^{2},

where the final inequality follows from
\mathbf{x}_{1}\triangleq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{n_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{V}_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
such that
\left\|\mathbf{x}_{1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{x}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\leq 2r
.

Finally, due to that
\mathbb{E}(f_{t}(S_{t}))\geq\mathbb{E}(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))
from
Theorem

5
, we can infer that
(\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\beta+\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}})
-Regret of our proposed
Algorithm

4
, i.e.,
\text{Reg}_{(\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\beta+\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}})}\left(T\right)
satisfies that

\displaystyle\text{Reg}_{(\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\beta+\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}})}\left(T\right)\triangleq\Big(\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\beta+\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}\Big)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S^{*})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S_{t})

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{1}{\beta+\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}\Big)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\gamma^{2}f_{t}(S^{*})-(\beta+\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2})F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\frac{1}{\beta+\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}}\Big)\Big(\frac{r}{\eta}+\frac{n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T}{2}\eta\Big)\leq\frac{4r}{3\eta}+\frac{2n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}T}{3}\eta=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{r}{\eta}+nT\eta\right),

where the final inequality comes from Lemma B.1 in
(
thiery2022two
)
, namely,
\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}\geq\frac{3}{4}
.

2)
We consider
Algorithm

4
with the auxiliary gradient estimation(See Lines 9-11). In other words, we choose
\text{Auxiliary}\triangleq\textbf{True}
and set
\boldsymbol{\mathrm{g}}_{t}\triangleq(\int_{a=0}^{1}w(a)\mathrm{d}a)\widehat{\nabla}F_{t}\big(z_{t}*\mathbf{x}_{t}\big)
where
z_{t}
is drawn from the random variable
\mathcal{Z}
with distribution
\text{Pr}(\mathcal{Z}\leq z)\triangleq\frac{\int_{0}^{z}w(a)\mathrm{d}a}{\int_{0}^{1}w(a)\mathrm{d}a},\forall z\in[0,1]
. As a result, from
Equation

43
, we can have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

(45)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\nabla F_{t}(z*\mathbf{x}_{t})\mathrm{d}z,\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}(\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\mathrm{d}z)^{2}\mathbb{E}\big(\|\widehat{\nabla}F_{t}\big(z_{t}*\mathbf{x}_{t}\big)\|_{2}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\nabla F_{t}(z*\mathbf{x}_{t})\mathrm{d}z,\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}(\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\mathrm{d}z)^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2},

where the final inequality follows from
Lemma

10
.

Then, if
i):
every set function
f_{t}:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is monotone and
\alpha
-weakly DR-submodular and
w(z)=e^{\alpha(z-1)}
, from
Lemma

1
and Eq.(
45
), we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\nabla F_{t}(z*\mathbf{x}_{t})\mathrm{d}z,\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}(\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\mathrm{d}z)^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-2\eta\Big((1-e^{-\alpha})f_{t}(S^{*})-F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big)+\eta^{2}(\frac{1-e^{-\alpha}}{\alpha})^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2},

where
S^{*}\triangleq\mathop{\arg\max}_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},|S\cup\mathcal{V}_{k}|\leq B_{k},\forall k\in[K]}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)
and the final inequality comes from
Lemma

1
and
\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\mathrm{d}z=\int_{0}^{1}e^{\alpha(z-1)}\mathrm{d}z=\frac{1-e^{-\alpha}}{\alpha}
.

As a result,

\displaystyle 2\eta\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big((1-e^{-\alpha})f_{t}(S^{*})-F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)-E\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{T+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)+T\eta^{2}(\frac{1-e^{-\alpha}}{\alpha})^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2r+T\eta^{2}(\frac{1-e^{-\alpha}}{\alpha})^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}\leq 2r+T\eta^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2},

where the second inequality follows from
\mathbf{x}_{1}\triangleq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{n_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{V}_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
such that
\left\|\mathbf{x}_{1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{x}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\leq 2r
and the final inequality comes from
\frac{1-e^{-\alpha}}{\alpha}\leq 1
when
\alpha\in(0,1]
.

Finally, due to that
\mathbb{E}(f_{t}(S_{t}))\geq\mathbb{E}(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))
from
Theorem

5
, we can infer that
(1-e^{-\alpha})
-Regret of our proposed
Algorithm

4
, i.e.,
\text{Reg}_{(1-e^{-\alpha})}\left(T\right)
satisfies that

\displaystyle\text{Reg}_{(1-e^{-\alpha})}\left(T\right)\triangleq(1-e^{-\alpha})\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S^{*})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S_{t})\leq\frac{r}{\eta}+\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}nT}{2}\eta=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{r}{\eta}+nT\eta\right).

Then, if
ii):
every set function
f_{t}:2^{\mathcal{V}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
is monotone
(\gamma,\beta)
-weakly submodular and
w(z)=e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}
with
\phi(\gamma,\beta)=\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}
, from
Lemma

1
and Eq.(
45
), we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}
