\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\langle\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\nabla F_{t}(z*\mathbf{x}_{t})\mathrm{d}z,\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\rangle\Big)+\eta^{2}(\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\mathrm{d}z)^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}-2\eta\Big(\big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\big)f_{t}(S^{*})-F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big)+\eta^{2}\Big(\frac{1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)}}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\Big)^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2},

where
S^{*}\triangleq\mathop{\arg\max}_{S\subseteq\mathcal{V},|S\cup\mathcal{V}_{k}|\leq B_{k},\forall k\in[K]}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)
and the final inequality comes from
Lemma

1
and
\int_{0}^{1}w(z)\mathrm{d}z=\int_{0}^{1}e^{\phi(\gamma,\beta)(z-1)}\mathrm{d}z=\frac{1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)}}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}
.

As a result,

\displaystyle 2\eta\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\big)f_{t}(S^{*})-F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)-E\Big(\left\|\mathbf{x}_{T+1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\Big)+T\eta^{2}(\frac{1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)}}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)})^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2r+T\eta^{2}(\frac{1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)}}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)})^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}\leq 2r+T\eta^{2}n\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2},

where the second inequality follows from
\mathbf{x}_{1}\triangleq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{n_{k}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{V}_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
and
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\in\prod_{k=1}^{K}\Delta_{n_{k}}
such that
\left\|\mathbf{x}_{1}-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{x}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{S\cap\mathcal{V}_{k}}}{B_{k}}\|_{2}^{2}\leq 2r
and the final inequality comes from Lemma B.1 in
(
thiery2022two
)
, namely,
\phi(\gamma,\beta)=\beta(1-\gamma)+\gamma^{2}\geq\frac{3}{4}
and the truth that
\frac{1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)}}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\leq 1
when
\phi(\gamma,\beta)\geq\frac{3}{4}
.

Finally, due to that
\mathbb{E}(f_{t}(S_{t}))\geq\mathbb{E}(F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))
from
Theorem

5
, we can infer that
\big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\big)
-Regret of our proposed
Algorithm

4
, i.e.,
\text{Reg}_{\big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\big)}\left(T\right)
satisfies that

\displaystyle\text{Reg}_{\big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\big)}\left(T\right)\triangleq\big(\frac{\gamma^{2}(1-e^{-\phi(\gamma,\beta)})}{\phi(\gamma,\beta)}\big)\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S^{*})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S_{t})\leq\frac{r}{\eta}+\frac{\bar{B}^{2}M_{1:T}^{2}nT}{2}\eta=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{r}{\eta}+nT\eta\right).

∎

G-B

Transferring the Online Results of
Theorem

9
into Offline Settings

In this subsection, we show how to transfer the online results of
Theorem

9
into offline settings throughout the operation in Line 16 of
Algorithm

4
. At first, from the details of
Theorem

9
, we know that, if we set the step size
\eta\triangleq\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\frac{r}{nT}})
, our
Algorithm

4
can achieve a
\rho
-regret bound as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\text{Reg}_{\rho}\left(T\right)\right)=\rho\max_{S\in\mathcal{C}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(f_{t}(S_{t})\right)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{r}{\eta}+nT\eta\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{nrT}\right),

where the obtained approximation ratio
\rho
depends on whether the auxiliary gradient estimation is employed.

Therefore, when every incoming set function
f_{t}
is a fixed set objective
f
, we can show that the subset
S_{l}\triangleq\mathop{\arg\max}_{S\in\{S_{1},\dots,S_{T}\}}f(S)
returned by the Line 16 of
Algorithm

4
satisfies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(f(S_{l}))\geq\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\big(f(S_{t})\big)}{T}=\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\big(f_{t}(S_{t})\big)}{T}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\rho\max_{S\in\mathcal{C}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(S)-\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{nrT}\right)}{T}=\rho\max_{S\in\mathcal{C}}f(S)-\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\frac{nr}{T}}).

Especially when
T=\mathcal{O}(\frac{nr}{\epsilon^{2}})
and
\eta\triangleq\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\frac{r}{nT}})=\mathcal{O}(\frac{\epsilon}{n})
, we can have
\mathbb{E}(f(S_{l}))\geq\rho\max_{S\in\mathcal{C}}f(S)-\epsilon
, which verifies the discussions in
Remark

17
.
